# Hamilton Beach: Road, Street and Right-of-Way Wording

October 11, 2026 checkpoint. This report separates express access wording from road boundaries, street-line restrictions, generic record clauses and beach passage; it is a documentary index, not a title opinion. The scan-by-scan pass is **incomplete**, with unread and defective captures listed explicitly.

## Coverage and limits

310 of 434 reference entries have completed road reviews, including12 new follow-up deeds. 112 entries remain pending and 12 have partial, missing-scan, blocked or wrong-header status. Of the original186 current-reference entries,176 have completed road reviews. Reference aliases, certificates and trusts occur, so these figures are not counts of distinct deeds. [Plymouth Registry](https://titleview.org/plymouthdeeds/)

No beach-right colors were changed by this road review. A street name in a boundary, an address, or a generic “subject to” clause is not reported as an express road-use grant; no public acceptance, road ownership or currently enforceable maintenance duty is inferred.

Construction clauses in Fresh Pond Estates, Plymouth (3147/127–129), street/sewer provisions for Hull (500/235), and the Fourth Avenue rights in Weweantic Shores (4345/49 and4862/466) concern other locations. They are retained as rejected chain-search evidence, not Hamilton Beach roadway rules. Trust-beneficiary property access is not automatically a road easement or road-maintenance agreement. [Plymouth Registry](https://titleview.org/plymouthdeeds/)

## Selected verified road wording

- **27 WORRALL AVE (37837/149, page149)**: “Together with a right of way in common with others over avenues and beach as laid out in said Plan.” Scope:Lot6 ShoreLotsQuasuettPlan2/640. Express plan-defined common ROW; does not prove public acceptance, ownership or precise mapped extent. [Plymouth Registry](https://titleview.org/plymouthdeeds/)
- **13 GLEN AVE (46826/187, page187)**: “This conveyance is made together with a right of way in common with others to use the beach for bathing, boating and passing and also a right of way to use all of the streets and avenues in common with others entitled thereto.” Scope:Lot73.. Express streets/avenuesROW;scopeofentitlementandpublicacceptancenotadjudicated. [Plymouth Registry](https://titleview.org/plymouthdeeds/)
- **9 WORRALL AVE (1688/569, page570)**: “Together with the right in common with others to use the beach for bathing, boating and passing and also the use of all streets and avenues.” Scope:Lots14/15; current9WorrallLot15 match; Lot16B expressly excluded.. Operative clause already visually verified in earlier title review. Full new road pass remains pending as shown by this row status; not a grant for Lot16B. [Plymouth Registry](https://titleview.org/plymouthdeeds/)
- **24 ALGELO AVE (47480/206, page206)**: “Together with the right in common with others to use the beach for bathing, boating and passing and also the use of all streets and avenues.” Scope:Lot38 Plan2/639; separately also Lot39 Plan3/511. Immediately follows Lot38 description on206; do not automatically extend to separately described Lot39 on207. Street extents and public status not established. [Plymouth Registry](https://titleview.org/plymouthdeeds/)
- **4 POINT RD / 50 PILGRIM AVE (48425/322, page322)**: “Together with the right of way over Point Road.” Scope:PARCEL ONE:Lot35Plan6/774only.. ExpressROWoverPointRoad;notautomaticgrantforbeachparcel2orLot36parcel3. [Plymouth Registry](https://titleview.org/plymouthdeeds/)
- **8 POINT RD (16152/96, page96)**: “The premises are subject to a right of way, to the extent same is still in force and effect, reserved by deed of Arthur L. Smith to A. Gordon Smith dated 23 December 1947 and recorded in Plymouth County Registry of Deeds Book 1995 Page 518 for the benefit of all the other lots shown on said plan over a strip of land 10 feet in width extending 10 feet westerly from the easterly line of the granted premises and running from said Point Road to the beach, and said right of way is limited to passage by foot.” Scope:BurdenedLot33QuasuetBluffsPlan6/774,easterly10-footstrip.. Foot passage only; conditional on continued force/effect; reservation favors other lots, not unrestricted vehicle access. [Plymouth Registry](https://titleview.org/plymouthdeeds/)
- **21 PILGRIM AVE (55443/245, page245)**: “Lot 5 is subject to a taking for a Street Layout for Pilgrim Avenue as recorded with said Registry of Deeds in Book 2043, Page 399.” Scope:QuasuetBluffs lot5. Recital of recorded streetlayout taking only; underlying taking not read here and no acceptance/ownership conclusion. [Plymouth Registry](https://titleview.org/plymouthdeeds/)
